# First owner-authorized neural training run — October 19, 2026

The owner explicitly authorized taking over and starting the configured Jupyter run. The completed run contains eight terminal games, zero caps, 588 learner decisions and seven PPO optimizer steps; KL early stopping operated normally. The neural checkpoint and its hash, optimizer state and changed weights were verified at `runs/neural_ppo/8917daca7a65408abf918ee8740b9dea/training-000001.pt`. No playing-strength claim follows from this starter run. GitHub remains untouched.

Two preceding attempts stopped before PPO updates. Repairs preserve learned minion definitions through Ascendance restoration and route weapon after-attack Discover through resumable event frames. Affected check groups passed (75, 38, 36 and 63 checks; overlapping groups are not additive unique coverage). The engine is now `245c5073fdcfd14eb82e68f1185720522e15984848a4a5622b386e0183b329c5`. The older 5,615-check full receipt does not validate this changed source; a full regression after these repairs remains pending. Details: `../../docs/engine-audit/neural-first-run-repairs-2026-10-19.json`.

# Neural trainer added — October 19, 2026

Notebook 15 now provides an owner-launched 4.76M-parameter Transformer policy/value model, PPO, four sourced fixed competitive decks and frozen checkpoint opponents. 19 non-training neural fixtures and 75 supporting checks passed; opening-position inference passed for all four lists without playing game actions. Notebook syntax/schema, dependency integrity, lint and the existing staged facade type checks passed. No training or GitHub action was started by this implementation work. End-to-end training and playing strength remain unmeasured. See `../../docs/NEURAL_TRAINING.md` and `../../docs/engine-audit/neural-ppo-implementation-2026-10-19.json`.

The simulator source fingerprint remains `1835ac39c646097302be3043ff3c11885ec629f8c3ec3933e61b08f14a160a56`; its prior 5,615-check non-training engine receipt remains current. It does not validate neural learning quality or certify exact game fidelity. Original linear experiments/models are preserved.

# Current implementation checkpoint — October 19, 2026

**107 of 117 audited coding entries are written/repaired; 10 remain open.** This batch implements 32 entries, including all 18 previously missing historical spell roots. These are work-item counts, not a percentage of game fidelity.

The registry contains 8,312 identities. Amber Warden has 3,200 eligible minion identities with bindings in all eleven classes; the one-identity reduction follows the documented bare-Zilliax generation ban, not an implementation filter. All 8,294 published operation occurrences now have structural operand contracts (zero uncovered names; original 515-name gap closed). Registration and structural validation do not certify semantic behavior.

**Full regressions are deferred until coding is finished, as requested.** Affected focused checks passed; their overlapping logs are retained separately. The previous 5,545-test full pass belongs to an older source and does not validate this batch. No training or GitHub actions. All 160 fidelity contracts remain tracked, along with broader policy-feature sufficiency.

Current source fingerprint: `77aa5714799adb3295ea8fca0f2bc7c788a394fedee86a2d4802d7fcadc567da`. Receipt: `docs/engine-audit/project-completion-audit/evidence/remaining-42-deferred-2026-10-19.json`.

The ten remaining entries:

- **Inherited physical relationship: muradin_return** (`ENGINE:inherit:muradin_return`): Copied Deathrattle relationship to one held physical Hammer, consumption and copied-bearer behavior not established.
- **Inherited physical relationship: hammer_shuffle** (`ENGINE:inherit:hammer_shuffle`): Inherited weapon Deathrattle on another bearer: actual returned physical card, permanent attack and repetition semantics not established.
- **Bashana allocation contract has no production default** (`SOURCE:bashana_contract`): Exact weighted allocation of the 12-mana Nature-spell budget among surviving Treants remains unknown.
- **Watfin suspicious-mutation contract has no production default** (`SOURCE:watfin_contract`): Exact mutation distribution and complete name/tribe mutation overlays remain unknown.
- **Vyranoth uneven distribution rejects** (`SOURCE:vyranoth_remainder`): Non-divisible +50/+50 remainder allocation and probabilities remain unknown.
- **Animal Companion upgrades reject empty successor costs** (`SOURCE:companion_cost_gap`): Frozen-patch successor behavior when no eligible Beast exists at exact next cost remains unestablished; reports of above-10 bugs do not determine general fallback.
- **Garona rejects multiple held King Llanes** (`SOURCE:king_llane_duplicates`): Garona selection among multiple held King Llanes, number destroyed and health-halving repetitions remain unestablished.
- **Muradin rejects binding a second physical Hammer** (`SOURCE:muradin_second_hammer`): Second Hammer while Muradin already holds one: replacement versus multiple physical attachments not established.
- **Free triggering of Rogue Imbued power rejects** (`SOURCE:rogue_free_imbue`): Free Wisprider trigger of Rogue power needs established Rewind semantics and checkpoint-safe implementation; generation body alone is not sufficient.
- **Camouflage Mount rejects an exhausted Bonus Effect pool** (`SOURCE:camouflage_exhausted`): No eligible Bonus Effect remains: token/Deathrattle/fallback behavior unestablished.
